- **np-kb-git-gotchas**: Gotchas when committing/inspecting changes with git via a shell (esp. the Bash tool) that lead to wrong commits or wrong conclusions. Chiefly — a file MOVE split across two commits is NOT detected as a rename, so a `D old/path` in `git status` can be a move (content still exists elsewhere), not a deletion; and backticks/`$(...)` inside a double-quoted `git commit -m "..."` trigger shell command substitution and silently mangle the message.
